See that step for why.\n47\t\n48\t**Explicit contract — propagate `--paths` through a single normalized\n49\tvariable.** Downstream steps (`spawn_agents`, `sequential_mapping`, and any\n50\tAgent-mode prompt construction) MUST use `${PATH_SCOPE_HINT}` to ensure every\n51\tmapper receives the same deterministic scope. Without this contract\n52\tincremental-remap can silently regress to a whole-repo scan.\n53\t\n54\t```bash\n55\t# Validated, comma-separated paths (empty if --paths absent or all rejected):\n56\tSCOPED_PATHS=\"<validated paths or empty>\"\n57\tif [ -n \"$SCOPED_PATHS\" ]; then\n58\t  PATH_SCOPE_HINT=\"--paths $SCOPED_PATHS\"\n59\telse\n60\t  PATH_SCOPE_HINT=\"\"\n61\tfi\n62\t```\n63\t\n64\tAll mapper prompts built later in this workflow MUST include\n65\t`${PATH_SCOPE_HINT}` (expanded to empty when full-repo mode is in effect).\n66\t\n67\tWhen `--paths` is absent, behave exactly as before: full-repo scan, all 7\n68\tdocuments refreshed.\n69\t</step>\n70\t\n71\t<step name=\"init_context\" priority=\"first\">\n72\tLoad codebase mapping context:\n73\t\n74\t```bash\n75\t_GSD_SHIM_NAME=\"gsd-tools.cjs\"; esac; }; _gsd_homes() { set -- \"${CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR:-$HOME/.claude}\" \"${ANTIGRAVITY_CONFIG_DIR:-$HOME/.gemini/antigravity}\" \"$HOME/.gemini/antigravity-ide\" \"$HOME/.gemini/antigravity-cli\" \"${AUGMENT_CONFIG_DIR:-$HOME/.augment}\" \"${CLINE_CONFIG_DIR:-$HOME/.cline}\" \"${CODEBUDDY_CONFIG_DIR:-$HOME/.codebuddy}\" \"${CODEX_HOME:-$HOME/.codex}\" \"${COPILOT_CONFIG_DIR:-${COPILOT_HOME:-$HOME/.copilot}}\" \"${CURSOR_CONFIG_DIR:-$HOME/.cursor}\" \"${HERMES_HOME:-$HOME/.hermes}\" \"${KILO_CONFIG_DIR:-${XDG_CONFIG_HOME:-$HOME/.config}/kilo}\" \"${KIMI_CONFIG_DIR:-$HOME/.config/agents}\" \"$HOME/.agents\" \"${KIMI_CODE_HOME:-$HOME/.kimi-code}\" \"${OPENCODE_CONFIG_DIR:-${XDG_CONFIG_HOME:-$HOME/.config}/opencode}\" \"${PI_CODING_AGENT_DIR:-$HOME/.pi/agent}\" \"${QWEN_CONFIG_DIR:-$HOME/.qwen}\" \"${TRAE_CONFIG_DIR:-$HOME/.trae}\" \"${WINDSURF_CONFIG_DIR:-$HOME/.codeium/windsurf}\" \"${ZCODE_CONFIG_DIR:-$HOME/.zcode}\" \"${GROK_AGENTS_HOME:-$HOME/.agents}\"; then gsd_run() { node \"$GSD_TOOLS\" \"$@\"; }; elif _gsd_homes; then gsd_run() { node \"$GSD_TOOLS\" \"$@\"; }; elif unset -f gsd_run; _G=\"$(command -v gsd_run)\"; [ -n \"$_G\" ] && _gsd_id_ok \"$_G\"; then GSD_TOOLS=\"$_G\"; gsd_run() { \"$GSD_TOOLS\" \"$@\"; }; else echo \"ERROR: gsd-tools.cjs not found at $GSD_TOOLS and no identity-proving gsd_run is on PATH. Run: npx -y @opengsd/gsd-core@latest --claude --local\" >&2; exit 1; fi; GSD_IDENTITY_STATUS=unverified; _gsd_id_ok gsd_run && GSD_IDENTITY_STATUS=ok; export GSD_IDENTITY_STATUS; [ \"$GSD_IDENTITY_STATUS\" = ok ] || echo \"WARNING: \\\"$GSD_TOOLS\\\" did not prove it is @opengsd/gsd-core - it is either a different package or an @opengsd/gsd-core older than the runtime-identity verb. On named-dispatch runtimes (Claude/OpenCode/…) the role is returned unchanged; on kimi-code it maps to `coder`/`explore`/`plan` by role-suffix. Wait for all 4 agents to complete before proceeding to collect_confirmations. This prevents duplicate work and wasted context.\n277\t\n278\tContinue to collect_confirmations.\n279\t</step>\n280\t\n281\t<step name=\"collect_confirmations\">\n282\tWait for all 4 background agents to finish, then read each agent's output file to collect confirmations.\n283\t\n284\tEach `Agent(...)` call above with `run_in_background=true` returns an `async_launched` result that carries an `outputFile` path (and `canReadOutputFile: true`). The 4 agents run concurrently and each one's completion arrives as a message in this conversation when it finishes — do NOT issue a separate blocking call to wait for them.\n285\t\n286\t**Once all 4 agents have reported completion, read each agent's output file (single message with 4 Read calls):**\n287\t```\n288\tRead tool:\n289\t  file_path: \"{outputFile from that agent's async_launched result}\"\n290\t```\n291\t\n292\t> Allow up to `workflow.subagent_timeout` for the slowest agent to finish before treating it as failed. Increase for large codebases or slower models.\n293\t\n294\tEach output file contains that agent's completion confirmation. Parse the confirmation marker (see below) from the file contents.\n295\t\n296\t**Expected confirmation format from each agent:**\n297\t```\n298\t## Mapping Complete\n299\t\n300\t**Focus:** {focus}\n301\t**Documents written:**\n302\t- `.planning/codebase/{DOC1}.md` ({N} lines)\n303\t- `.planning/codebase/{DOC2}.md` ({N} lines)\n304\t\n305\tReady for orchestrator summary.\n306\t```\n307\t\n308\t**What you receive:** Just file paths and line counts. This replaces `spawn_agents` and `collect_confirmations`.\n317\t\n318\t**IMPORTANT:** Do NOT use `browser_subagent`, `Explore`, or any browser-based tool. Use only file system tools (Read, Bash, Write, Grep, Glob, list_dir, view_file, grep_search, or equivalent tools available in your runtime).\n319\t\n320\t**IMPORTANT:** Set all date stamps (`**Analysis Date:**`, footer, `<!-- refreshed -->`) to `{date}` from init context, overwriting any existing date — Update runs seed from files with concrete prior dates, so merely replacing `[YYYY-MM-DD]` placeholders is not sufficient. Include actual file paths formatted with backticks.\n346\t\n347\tContinue to verify_output.\n348\t</step>\n349\t\n350\t<step name=\"verify_output\">\n351\tVerify all documents created successfully:\n352\t\n353\t```bash\n354\tls -la .planning/codebase/\n355\twc -l .planning/codebase/*.md\n356\t```\n357\t\n358\t**Verification checklist:**\n359\t- All 7 documents exist\n360\t- No empty documents (each should have >20 lines)\n361\t\n362\tIf any documents missing or empty, note which agents may have failed.\n363\t\n364\tContinue to stamp_codebase_map.\n365\t</step>\n366\t\n367\t<step name=\"stamp_codebase_map\">\n368\tStamp the drift baseline into every document that was just written:\n369\t\n370\t```bash\n371\tgsd_run stamp-codebase-map ${UPDATED_DOCS:+--files \"$UPDATED_DOCS\"}\n372\t```\n373\t\n374\tThis writes `last_mapped_commit: <HEAD sha>` and `last_mapped_at: <date>` into\n375\tthe YAML frontmatter of each `.planning/codebase/*.md` that exists. It runs on\n376\tevery mapping run, incremental (`--paths`) and full alike. `--files` narrows it\n377\tto the documents an Update run actually refreshed; `--paths` needs no narrowing\n378\tbecause all seven are regenerated, just scoped in content.\n379\t\n380\t**Why this is a shell step and not an instruction to the mapper.** The stamp is\n381\tthe only machine-readable freshness marker: the `verify codebase-drift` gate\n382\treads it to decide what to diff HEAD against. The human-readable markers the\n383\tmapper writes (`**Analysis Date:**`, `<!-- refreshed: ... -->`) are restamped\n384\tunconditionally on an Update run, so a mapper that decides its work is already\n385\tdone and rewrites only the dates still looks fresh to a human. Leaving the\n386\tmachine-readable stamp to the same agent reproduces exactly the failure the\n387\tstamp exists to detect. A shell step cannot be skipped by a confident agent.\n388\t\n389\tThe command is non-blocking: it emits `skipped` with a `reason` outside a git\n390\trepo or when no documents exist. Report `stamped` and `commit` in the summary\n391\tif any entry in `failed` is non-empty; otherwise continue silently.\n392\t\n393\tRun in this position, before `commit_codebase_map`, the stamp lands on\n394\tdocuments the mapper just wrote, so its markdown whitespace normalization is\n395\tfolded into the same commit. Running `stamp-codebase-map` by hand against an\n396\talready-committed map reflows that map's whitespace as a side effect.\n397\t\n398\tContinue to scan_for_secrets.\n399\t</step>\n400\t\n401\t<step name=\"scan_for_secrets\">\n402\t**CRITICAL SECURITY CHECK:** Scan output files for accidentally leaked secrets before committing.\n403\t\n404\tRun secret pattern detection:\n405\t\n406\t```bash\n407\t# Check for common API key patterns in generated docs\n408\tgrep -E '(sk-[a-zA-Z0-9]{20,}|sk_live_[a-zA-Z0-9]+|sk_test_[a-zA-Z0-9]+|ghp_[a-zA-Z0-9]{36}|gho_[a-zA-Z0-9]{36}|glpat-[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+|AKIA[A-Z0-9]{16}|xox[baprs]-[a-zA-Z0-9-]+|[REDACTED:gsd-scan]|eyJ[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+\\.eyJ[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+\\.)' .planning/codebase/*.md 2>/dev/null && SECRETS_FOUND=true || SECRETS_FOUND=false\n```\n\n**If SECRETS_FOUND=true:**\n\n```\n⚠️  SECURITY ALERT: Potential secrets detected in codebase documents!\n\nFound patterns that look like API keys or tokens in:\n[show grep output]\n\nThis would expose credentials if committed.\n\n**Action required:**\n1. Review the flagged content above\n2. If these are real secrets, they must be removed before committing\n3. Consider adding sensitive files to Claude Code \"Deny\" permissions\n\nPausing before commit. Reply \"safe to proceed\" if the flagged content is not actually sensitive, or edit the files first.\n```\n\nWait for user confirmation before continuing to commit_codebase_map.\n\n**If SECRETS_FOUND=false:**\n\nContinue to commit_codebase_map.\n</step>\n\n<step name=\"commit_codebase_map\">\nCommit the codebase map:\n\n```bash\ngsd_run query commit \"docs: map existing codebase\" --files .planning/codebase/*.md\n```\n\nContinue to offer_next.\n</step>\n\n<step name=\"offer_next\">\nPresent completion summary and next steps.\n\n**Get line counts:**\n```bash\nwc -l .planning/codebase/*.md\n```\n\n**Output format:**\n\n```\nCodebase mapping complete.\n\nCreated .planning/codebase/:\n- STACK.md ([N] lines) - {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01NkfvFBByS8zkrn4VVJwWhy","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# Architecture\n2\t\n3\tStatus: Accepted\n4\t\n5\tThis document describes how the portfolio site is structured: layers, import rules, folder layout, data flow, the content model, the design-token pipeline and the animation layer. It is written so the scaffold can be built directly from it. Decisions with a longer rationale live in the [ADR log](adr/README.md).\n6\t\n7\t## Overview\n8\t\n9\tThe site is a static, zero-backend, single-page React app served from GitHub Pages. There is no router, no global store and no data fetching. The architecture comes down to four seams:\n10\t\n11\t1. Typed content: all copy lives in TypeScript modules checked against one schema.\n12\t2. CSS-variable design tokens: five role colors per theme drive every surface.\n13\t3. An internal animation layer: the only place the two animation libraries are imported.\n14\t4. A build and deploy pipeline: Vite build, GitHub Actions, GitHub Pages.\n15\t\n16\tEverything else is conventional React composition with props flowing down.\n17\t\n18\t## Layers\n19\t\n20\tDependencies point down only. A layer may import from layers below it, never above.\n21\t\n22\t```text\n23\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n24\t| app/        main.tsx, App.tsx, Providers, Page.tsx                    |\n25\t|             Page.tsx is the ONLY file that imports content data       |\n26\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n27\t| sections/   Hero, About, Experience, Projects, Skills, Education,     |\n28\t|             Contact                      (props in, JSX out)          |\n29\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n30\t| layout/     Container, Section, SiteHeader, PillNav, Footer,          |\n31\t|             SkipLink, Wordmark                                        |\n32\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n33\t| components/ ui/ (CtaLink, Toast, ThemeToggle, icons)                  |\n34\t|             motion-primitives/ (vendored source, treated as owned)    |\n35\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n36\t| anim/       the internal animation layer: the only place that         |\n37\t|             imports motion/react and @react-spring/web               |\n38\t+-------------------------------+--------------------------------------+\n39\t| hooks/  lib/                  | styles/ (tokens.css, base.css,       |\n40\t| (useActiveSection, useTheme,  |  index.css = tailwind + theme map)   |\n41\t|  cn, a11y)                    |                                      |\n42\t+-------------------------------+--------------------------------------+\n43\t| content/ (typed TS data)      assets/ (images, fonts via Fontsource) |\n44\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n45\tBuild and deploy: vite.config.ts, lint boundary rules, GitHub Actions\n46\t```\n47\t\n48\t## Import rules\n49\t\n50\tOnly src/anim/ and src/components/motion-primitives/ may import motion/react or @react-spring/web.\n51\t\n52\tOnly src/app/Page.tsx imports content data.\n53\t\n54\t| Importer | May import | May not import |\n55\t|----------|-----------|----------------|\n56\t| sections/* | layout, components/ui, the anim barrel (src/anim/index.ts), content types (not data) | motion/react, @react-spring/*, components/motion-primitives/* directly, content data |\n57\t| layout/*, components/ui/* | the anim barrel, lib, hooks | sections, content data |\n58\t| components/motion-primitives/* | motion/react, lib/cn | everything else (vendor code, kept out of lint autofix churn) |\n59\t| anim/* | motion/react, @react-spring/web, components/motion-primitives/*, the token mirror in styles | sections, layout |\n60\t| app/Page.tsx | content data, sections | any animation library or anim internals |\n61\t\n62\tThese rules are enforced by oxlint's `no-restricted-imports` rule with per-folder overrides in `.oxlintrc.json`, covering dynamic `import()` and re-exports as well, so a violation fails CI rather than relying on review. Unit tests under `tests/unit` may import the libraries for assertions. A gesture library is not adopted up front. It would be added to the anim row only if a react-spring drag cannot be built well otherwise, after a React 19 compatibility check.\n63\t\n64\t## Folder structure\n65\t\n66\t```text\n67\trepo root\n68\t|-- public/                  URL-stable files only: favicon set and\n69\t|                            resume.pdf (a phone-number-free export); the og\n70\t|                            image and robots.txt are launch assets, not yet\n71\t|                            shipped\n72\t|-- index.html               meta description, inline theme-init script and the\n73\t|                            static hero shell; social tags and noscript\n74\t|                            content for the other sections arrive with the\n75\t|                            launch assets\n76\t|-- 404.html                 second HTML entry: the not-found page that\n77\t|                            GitHub Pages serves for unknown paths\n78\t|-- vite.config.ts           base from env, Tailwind plugin, imagetools, \"@\" alias\n79\t|-- .oxlintrc.json           import-boundary rules (see Import rules)\n80\t|-- .github/workflows/       ci.yml, deploy.yml\n81\t`-- src/\n82\t    |-- main.tsx\n83\t    |-- app/                 App.tsx, Page.tsx, Providers.tsx\n84\t    |-- sections/            one folder per section (Hero, About, Experience,\n85\t    |                        Projects, Skills, Education, Contact)\n86\t    |-- layout/              Container, Section, SiteHeader, PillNav,\n87\t    |                        Footer, SkipLink, Wordmark\n88\t    |-- components/\n89\t    |   |-- ui/              CtaLink, Toast, ThemeToggle, icons/ (inline SVG\n90\t    |   |                    brand marks and UI glyphs)\n91\t    |   `-- motion-primitives/   vendored and logged in an ADR (TextEffect, kept\n92\t    |                        unrendered for the hero role-line effect)\n93\t    |-- anim/\n94\t    |   |-- index.ts         public barrel: the only animation import path\n95\t    |   |-- MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx\n96\t    |   |-- useMotionPreference.ts\n97\t    |   |-- tokens.ts        springs, durations, eases in both libraries' formats\n98\t    |   |-- MotionFeatures.tsx   LazyMotion provider with the async feature chunk\n99\t    |   |-- declarative/     motion-owned wrappers (ToastPresence,\n100\t    |   |                    NavIndicator, PillMorph)\n101\t    |   `-- physics/         react-spring-owned wrappers (ThemeThumb and other\n102\t    |                        pointer-driven, velocity-carrying helpers)\n103\t    |-- content/\n104\t    |   |-- schema.ts        every type\n105\t    |   |-- profile.ts, experience.ts, projects.ts, skills.ts,\n106\t    |   |   education.ts, contacts.ts     one module per section\n107\t    |   |-- images.ts        image registry\n108\t    |   `-- index.ts         assembles siteContent with satisfies\n109\t    |-- hooks/               useActiveSection, useTheme\n110\t    |-- lib/                 cn.ts, date formatting, a11y helpers\n111\t    |-- styles/\n112\t    |   |-- index.css        tailwind import, dark variant, theme mapping\n113\t    |   |-- tokens.css       role tokens, derived tokens, light and dark blocks\n114\t    |   `-- base.css         resets, focus ring, reduced-motion guard, fonts\n115\t    `-- assets/images/       hashed by Vite\n116\t```\n117\t\n118\tThe animation folder is named anim/ rather than motion/ so it is never confused with the motion package or its motion/react import path.\n119\t\n120\t## Data flow\n121\t\n122\tBuild time:\n123\t\n124\t```text\n125\tcontent/*.ts --(typed, satisfies)--> app/Page --props--> sections --props--> layout, ui\n126\tassets/*     --(imagetools, hashed imports)--> image fields in content\n127\t```\n128\t\n129\tRuntime, all client-side with no network use beyond same-origin assets:\n130\t\n131\t- Theme: an inline script in the document head reads localStorage, falls back to prefers-color-scheme, and sets the data-theme attribute on the html element before first paint. The theme provider in React only changes the attribute and storage on a user toggle.\n132\t- Motion preference: the OS reduced-motion setting (plus an optional site override) feeds one hook, useMotionPreference, which configures both animation libraries and the wrapper components. See Reduced motion.\n133\t- Scroll-spy: an IntersectionObserver over the section ids drives useActiveSection, which highlights the current section in the navigation.\n134\t- Pointer and touch input drive react-spring values written straight to the DOM, with no React re-render per frame.\n135\t- Project detail: a click or tap sets local state in the Projects section, which opens an overlay with focus handling.\n136\t\n137\tThere is no global state library. Three small contexts exist: theme, motion preference and, optionally, the active section. Everything else is props.\n138\t\n139\t## Content model\n140\t\n141\tAll types live in src/content/schema.ts:\n142\t\n143\t- Profile: name, tagline, bio paragraphs, optional quote, photo (ImageAsset), contacts.\n144\t- ContactLink: kind (email, github, linkedin or instagram), label, href, whether it is external.\n145\t- ExperienceEntry: id, role, org, optional org URL, optional logo, dates (DateRange), location, bullets, optional highlights and stack.\n146\t- Project: id, title, context kind (personal, employer, course or hackathon) with a label, dates, stack, bullets, relatedExperienceId, featured, optional links and image.\n147\t- EducationEntry: id, degree, school, location, dates, gpa, optional track.\n148\t- SkillGroup: id, label, items as a string array.\n149\t- DateRange: ISO start and end (or present) plus a display label in one normalized format.\n150\t- ImageAsset: src, alt, width, height, optional srcset.\n151\t\n152\tRules:\n153\t\n154\t- Content modules use satisfies against the schema so a missing field fails typecheck.\n155\t- Ordering lives in the data (array order or a priority field), never in components.\n156\t- Copy is taken verbatim from the reviewed content inventory; any edit is recorded there with before and after, not made silently in code.\n157\t- The featured flag decides what appears first on mobile, so \"key info in seconds\" is a data decision.\n158\t- Content tests check that ids are unique, related ids resolve, every image has alt text, every external link is https, and dates parse in descending order.\n159\t- A phone number is not a field anywhere in the model, and must never appear in site copy, metadata or the served resume.\n160\t\n161\t## Design tokens\n162\t\n163\t- Exactly five role tokens per theme: text, background, primary, secondary, accent. Light and dark are authored separately, not inverted.\n164\t- Derived tokens (surface, border, muted text, link, focus ring and so on) are computed from the five roles with color-mix(in oklch, ...). A palette swap therefore edits one block and every derived value follows.\n165\t- The theme is selected by the data-theme attribute on the html element.\n166\t- Tailwind v4 is configured CSS-first: a custom dark variant is bound to [data-theme=dark] with @custom-variant, and an @theme inline block maps utilities to the CSS variables. There is no tailwind.config.js.\n167\t- No raw hex values in components. Components use semantic utilities only; a CI check greps for stray hex.\n168\t- A contrast test recomputes every text, UI and focus pair in both themes against WCAG 2.2 AA (4.5:1 for body text, 3:1 for large text and UI) and fails the build on any miss.\n169\t- Spring and duration values that JavaScript needs are mirrored in CSS variables, with src/anim/tokens.ts as the canonical source for springs.\n170\t\n171\t## Animation layer\n172\t\n173\tsrc/anim/index.ts is the only animation import path for the rest of the app. It exposes a handful of semantic components and hooks, not library primitives.\n174\t\n175\t| Interaction class | Owner |\n176\t|-------------------|-------|\n177\t| Mount and unmount, presence, exit animations | motion |\n178\t| Layout and shared-element transitions | motion |\n179\t| Scroll-linked values and in-view reveals | motion |\n180\t| Text and number effects, stagger, ready-made motion-primitives prefabs | motion |\n181\t| Velocity-carrying physics: press-squish, rail hover lift with neighbor shift, theme toggle thumb | react-spring |\n182\t| Color and hover transitions, native scroll-snap rail swipe | CSS |\n183\t| Desktop wheel smoothing only | Lenis |\n184\t\n185\tThe rule: one library animates any given element. Never both libraries on one element or one property, and no Tailwind transition utilities on an element a library animates. If a vendored primitive duplicates a physics wrapper, one implementation is chosen per interaction and the other stays unused.\n186\t\n187\tSpring and duration tokens are defined once in src/anim/tokens.ts, exported in both libraries' formats (motion's stiffness and damping, react-spring's tension and friction), so the two feel like one system. Fine-pointer effects such as tilt, magnetic pull and spotlight are gated by (hover: hover) and (pointer: fine); touch gets a tap or press equivalent, or nothing. Every wrapper reads the motion preference, renders an instant equivalent when reduced, forwards className, and never hides content in its initial CSS state.\n188\t\n189\t## Reduced motion\n190\t\n191\tOne preference source, useMotionPreference, combines the OS prefers-reduced-motion setting with an optional site override and feeds three consumers:\n192\t\n193\t- motion: the provider renders MotionConfig with its reducedMotion setting. This disables transform and layout animation but not opacity or color, so wrappers handle parallax, scroll-linked and autoplay effects explicitly.\n194\t- react-spring: the provider calls Globals.assign({ skipAnimation }) so every spring jumps to its target; the effect cleans up on change.\n195\t- CSS: a @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) guard in base.css shortens transitions and animations and keeps scroll behavior native.\n196\t\n197\tContent is visible by default and is never hidden until JavaScript runs, and no wrapper applies a hidden initial state in CSS. Pressed, focused and selected states must read without motion (color, outline, shape). Lenis is off under reduced motion. The provider listens for changes to the media query, so a live OS toggle takes effect without a reload in both directions. Turning reduced motion off mid-visit also loads the motion feature chunk, which reduced-motion visitors otherwise never download.\n198\t\n199\t## Assets\n200\t\n201\t- Images live in src/assets/images/ and are imported as modules (hashed, long-cached). vite-imagetools produces AVIF and WebP variants and srcset with intrinsic width and height to prevent layout shift. Below-the-fold images lazy-load; the hero image is high priority.\n202\t- Fonts are self-hosted through Fontsource (variable, latin subset, font-display swap), at most two families. No third-party font CDN.\n203\t- Brand icons (GitHub, LinkedIn, Instagram, email) and UI glyphs (close, chevrons, download) are inline SVG components under src/components/ui/icons/. No icon package is installed. See ADR-0005 and ADR-0010 in the ADR log.\n204\t- The resume PDF served from public/ is the owner's phone-number-free export of the resume. The phone-number gate reads its text layer, link annotations and metadata, and rejects a stand-in file.\n205\t- Only images that are actually used migrate from the previous site, with EXIF metadata stripped. Template leftovers are left behind.\n206\t\n207\t## Build and deploy\n208\t\n209\tGitHub Actions builds the site and publishes it to GitHub Pages. The Vite base comes from the VITE_BASE environment variable: the project URL path (for example /aa-phan-portfolio-v2/) while staging, and / after the cutover to the user-site URL. Assets are module-imported so no absolute asset paths exist; anything under public/ that JavaScript references goes through import.meta.env.BASE_URL. CI also builds with the project-path base so a broken sub-path is caught before launch. The build has a second HTML entry, 404.html, a static not-found page that GitHub Pages serves for unknown paths. Steps, settings, cutover and rollback are in the deploy runbook (deploy-runbook.md).\n210\t\n211\t## Quality gates\n212\t\n213\t| Gate | Command | Fails the build when |\n214\t|------|---------|----------------------|\n215\t| Typecheck | `tsc -b` | A content field is missing or a reference is wrong |\n216\t| Lint with import boundaries | oxlint, warnings denied | A section imports an animation library or content data |\n217\t| Format check | Prettier with Tailwind class sorting | A file is not formatted |\n218\t| Unit tests | Vitest: shell parity, theme logic, motion preference, gate negatives | Any test fails |\n219\t| Path-case check | `scripts/check-path-case.mjs`, run locally and on Linux CI | An import differs in case from the file on disk |\n220\t| Raw color check | `scripts/check-colors.mjs` | Source outside the token file and vendored primitives holds a raw hex, rgb, hsl, oklch or oklab value, or a Tailwind default palette utility |\n221\t| Token contrast test | `scripts/check-contrast.mjs` | Any text, UI or focus pair is below AA in any theme |\n222\t| Build | `vite build` | The Vite build errors |\n223\t| Initial JS budget | `scripts/check-js-budget.mjs` | The module script and modulepreloads exceed 150 kB gzipped |\n224\t| Phone-number check | `scripts/check-no-phone.mjs` over the built site | A served file holds a phone-shaped string or a tel: link, or a PDF's text layer, link annotations or metadata does (read with pdfjs-dist), or the PDF cannot be read, or resume.pdf is not a real resume (the owner's name and at least 1500 non-whitespace characters of text) |\n225\t| Image check | `scripts/check-images.mjs` over dist, public and src | A shipped raster is not AVIF or WebP, carries Exif or XMP, or is unreferenced, or a source image is not imported |\n226\t| Smoke tests | Playwright in CI | The hero is missing with JavaScript off, the theme flashes, or content is hidden or a transform animation runs with reduced motion emulated |\n227\t| axe | Later gate | Serious or critical violations in light or dark |\n228\t| Mobile Lighthouse | Later gate | Targets set in the scaffold milestone are missed |\n229\t\n230\t`npm run verify` chains the first eleven rows in that order, and both CI workflows call it.\n231\t\n232\t## Stack\n233\t\n234\tVerified 2026-10-08; re-verify at scaffold.\n235\t\n236\t| Technology | Version | Purpose |\n237\t|------------|---------|---------|\n238\t| Vite | ^8.3.4 | Build and dev server |\n239\t| React and React DOM | ^19.3.0 | UI |\n240\t| TypeScript | ~6.0.3 | Types |\n241\t| @vitejs/plugin-react | ^6.1.2 | React transform |\n242\t| Tailwind CSS and @tailwindcss/vite | ^4.3.3 | Styling, first-party Vite plugin |\n243\t| motion | ~14.0.0 | Declarative animation, imported from motion/react |\n244\t| @react-spring/web | ~10.1.2 | Spring physics |\n245\t| clsx | ^2.1.1 | Class composition |\n246\t| tailwind-merge | ^3.7.0 | Class conflict resolution |\n247\t| lenis | 1.3.x | Desktop wheel smoothing |\n248\t| vite-imagetools | ^12.0.1 | Image resize and format variants |\n249\t| pdfjs-dist | 6.3.289 (exact, dev only) | Icon: `src/components/ui/icons/InstagramIcon.tsx`.\n  - Contact link typing: `ContactLink` in `src/content/schema.ts`. External links are flagged with `external: true`.\n  - Rule from `docs/architecture.md`: every external link must be `https`. Enforced by the content tests in `tests/unit/content.test.ts`.\n\n**Email (mailto, no provider):**\n- `mailto:` link to the owner's address, sourced from the `kind: 'email'` entry in `src/content/contacts.ts`. `src/app/Page.tsx` derives the address by slicing the `mailto:` prefix and passes it to `Hero` and `Contact`.\n- Copy-to-clipboard behavior: `src/components/ui/useCopyEmail.ts`. Used by `src/sections/Contact/Contact.tsx` and the hero CTA. Covered by `tests/e2e/copy-email.spec.ts`.\n- Email is not a form and no form service is used. `.claude/CLAUDE.md` records \"no backend\" as out of scope.\n\n**Static assets served from the origin:**\n- `public/resume.pdf` - Resume download link in `index.html` (`href=\"%BASE_URL%resume.pdf\"`, `download` attribute) and in the hero section. Validated by `scripts/check-no-phone.mjs`.\n- `public/favicon.svg` - Favicon, referenced from `index.html`.\n- Images under `src/assets/images/` (`about-dj.jpg`, `capitalone.png`, `spectrum.png`, `symbotic.png`) are imported as modules and processed by `vite-imagetools` at build time. Registry: `src/content/images.ts`.\n\n**Fonts:**\n- `@fontsource-variable/archivo` - Self-hosted npm package. Imported in `src/main.tsx`. No third-party font CDN (`docs/architecture.md`, Assets).\n\n**Analytics, tracking, and monitoring:** Not detected. No Google Analytics, Plausible, Sentry, or similar in `package.json`, `index.html`, or `src/`.\n\n## Data Storage\n\n**Databases:**\n- Not applicable. There is no database, ORM, or API client.\n\n**File Storage:**\n- Local filesystem only, at build time. Source assets live in `src/assets/images/` and `public/`. Build output goes to `dist/` (gitignored).\n\n**Caching:**\n- Browser `localStorage` only, for the theme preference under the key `theme` (`src/lib/theme.ts`, `THEME_STORAGE_KEY`). Read by the inline script in `index.html` before first paint and by `src/lib/theme.ts`. Storage access is wrapped in try/catch because storage can be blocked.\n- Service worker / HTTP cache policy: none configured in the repo. GitHub Pages sets the response headers.\n\n## Authentication & Identity\n\n**Auth Provider:**\n- Not applicable. The site has no login, sessions, or user accounts.\n\n**Identity in CI:**\n- GitHub OIDC token via `id-token: write`, scoped only to the `deploy` job in `.github/workflows/deploy.yml`. Used by `actions/deploy-pages` for Pages deployment. No long-lived secret is referenced in the workflows.\n\n## Monitoring & Observability\n\n**Error Tracking:**\n- None.\n\n**Logs:**\n- None in the app. Build and verify output goes to the CI log. `scripts/*.mjs` write to stdout/stderr when run.\n\n**Quality gates that act as observability:**\n- `scripts/check-no-phone.mjs` - Scans `dist` for phone-shaped strings and `tel:` links, and reads `public/resume.pdf` with `pdfjs-dist`.\n- `scripts/check-images.mjs` - Checks shipped rasters for AVIF/WebP format and EXIF/XMP metadata.\n- `scripts/check-js-budget.mjs` - Enforces the 150 kB gzipped initial JS budget.\n- `scripts/check-contrast.mjs` and `scripts/check-colors.mjs` - Token contrast and raw-color enforcement.\n\n## CI/CD & Deployment\n\n**Hosting:**\n- GitHub Pages. After cutover, the base is `/`.\n\n**CI Pipeline:**\n- GitHub Actions.\n  - `.github/workflows/ci.yml` - Runs on every `push` and `pull_request`. Job `verify`: `npm ci`, `npm run verify`, `npx playwright install --with-deps chromium`, `npm run test:e2e`.\n  - `.github/workflows/deploy.yml` - Runs on push to `main` and on `workflow_dispatch`. Jobs: `build` (verify and upload `./dist` artifact), `e2e` (Playwright), `deploy` (`actions/deploy-pages`). refreshed: 2026-11-20 -->\n# Architecture\n\n**Analysis Date:** 2026-11-20\n\n## System Overview\n\n```text\n┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│  Build time: Vite (vite.config.ts)                                    │\n│  content/*.ts (typed, satisfies schema)  +  assets/ (imagetools)      │\n└───────────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────┘\n                                │ static dist/ (index.html, 404.html)\n                                ▼\n┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│  Browser: single-page React app                                       │\n├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n│  Entry           `src/main.tsx` → `src/app/App.tsx`                   │\n│  Providers       `src/app/Providers.tsx`                              │\n│                  MotionPreferenceProvider → MotionFeatures →          │\n│                  ToastProvider                                        │\n├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n│  Page (only content consumer)  `src/app/Page.tsx`                     │\n│    SiteHeader + PillNav (scroll-spy)  │  Footer                       │\n│    Hero · Experience · Projects · About · Skills · Education · Contact │\n│    `src/sections/*`                                                   │\n├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n│  layout/ (`src/layout/*`)   components/ui/ (`src/components/ui/*`)    │\n├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n│  anim/ barrel (`src/anim/index.ts`) ── motion/react, @react-spring    │\n│  components/motion-primitives/ (vendored TextEffect)                  │\n├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n│  hooks/ (`src/hooks/*`)  lib/ (`src/lib/*`)  styles/ (`src/styles/*`) │\n└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n        Runtime writes only: html[data-theme], localStorage \"theme\",\n        and same-origin assets. No network calls.\n```\n\n## Component Responsibilities\n\n| Component | Responsibility | File |\n|-----------|----------------|------|\n| Entry | Mounts React root inside `StrictMode`, imports Archivo font and global CSS | `src/main.tsx` |\n| App | Composes `Providers` around `Page` | `src/app/App.tsx` |\n| Providers | Wraps motion preference, lazy motion features, and toast context | `src/app/Providers.tsx` |\n| Page | Reads `siteContent`, derives email, passes props to every section, runs the one scroll-spy | `src/app/Page.tsx` |\n| Content assembly | Exports the single `siteContent` object | `src/content/index.ts` |\n| Schema | All content types (`ImageAsset`, `DateRange`, `ContactLink`, `Profile`, `ExperienceEntry`, `Project`, `EducationEntry`, `SkillGroup`, `Writeup`) | `src/content/schema.ts` |\n| Scroll-spy | IntersectionObserver band, click lock, hash deep-link | `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts` |\n| Theme store | `useSyncExternalStore` over `html[data-theme]` | `src/hooks/useTheme.ts`, `src/lib/theme.ts` |\n| Motion preference | Combines OS reduced-motion with optional site override; configures motion and react-spring | `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts`, `src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx` |\n| Lazy motion features | `LazyMotion` boundary; loads `motion` feature bundle on demand | `src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx`, `src/anim/features.ts` |\n| Animation tokens | Springs and durations for both libraries | `src/anim/tokens.ts` |\n| Header and nav | Desktop sticky header with indicator | `src/layout/SiteHeader.tsx`, `src/layout/nav-items.ts` |\n| Phone pill nav | Mobile pill navigation with morph | `src/layout/PillNav.tsx` |\n| Toast | Context and presence-animated toast | `src/components/ui/Toast.tsx`, `src/components/ui/useToast.ts` |\n| Copy email | Clipboard hook used by Hero and Contact | `src/components/ui/useCopyEmail.ts` |\n| Projects dialog | Native `<dialog>` project detail overlay (ADR-0009) | `src/sections/Projects/ProjectDialog.tsx` |\n\n## Pattern Overview\n\n**Overall:** Static, zero-backend, single-page React application with a layered, props-down component tree and typed content modules.\n\n**Key Characteristics:**\n- No router. The page is one scroll document; sections are anchored by `id` (`SECTION_IDS` in `src/layout/nav-items.ts`).\n- No global state library. Three small contexts exist (theme via `useSyncExternalStore`, motion preference, toast). Everything else flows through props.\n- Content is data, not code. Components do not hard-code copy; ordering lives in array order or a `featured` flag in the data (`docs/architecture.md`, Content model).\n- Build-time validation: `satisfies` against `src/content/schema.ts` makes a missing field a typecheck failure.\n- Animation is isolated behind one barrel. Only `src/anim/` and `src/components/motion-primitives/` may import `motion/react` or `@react-spring/web`.\n- The app is fully client-side rendered from `index.html`. The static HTML contains a hero shell and section headings so the page has content before JS runs (`index.html`).\n\n## Layers\n\n**App (composition root):**\n- Purpose: Wires providers and the single page.\n- Location: `src/app/`\n- Contains: `main` entry is `src/main.tsx`; `App.tsx`, `Providers.tsx`, `Page.tsx`.\n- Depends on: `src/anim` barrel, `src/components/ui/Toast`, `src/content` (Page only), `src/hooks`, `src/layout`, `src/sections`.\n- Used by: `src/main.tsx` only.\n\n**Sections (presentation for each content area):**\n- Purpose: Render one content area from props.\n- Location: `src/sections/<Name>/<Name>.tsx`, with a per-section folder (`Projects/` also holds `ProjectDialog.tsx`, `Sleeve.tsx`, `project-types.ts`).\n- Contains: `Hero`, `Experience`, `Projects`, `About`, `Skills`, `Education`, `Contact`.\n- Depends on: `src/layout`, `src/components/ui`, the `src/anim` barrel, content types (not data).\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx`.\n\n**Layout:**\n- Purpose: Page chrome and reusable sectioning primitives.\n- Location: `src/layout/`\n- Contains: `Container.tsx`, `Section.tsx`, `SiteHeader.tsx`, `PillNav.tsx`, `Footer.tsx`, `SkipLink.tsx`, `Wordmark.tsx`, `nav-items.ts`.\n- Depends on: `src/anim` barrel, `src/hooks`, `src/lib`.\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx`, sections.\n\n**UI components:**\n- Purpose: Reusable controls and inline SVG glyphs.\n- Location: `src/components/ui/`\n- Contains: `CtaLink.tsx`, `ThemeToggle.tsx`, `Toast.tsx`, `useToast.ts`, `useCopyEmail.ts`, and `icons/` (inline SVG brand marks and glyphs, ADR-0005 and ADR-0010).\n- Depends on: `src/anim` barrel, `src/lib`, `src/hooks`.\n\n**Vendored primitives:**\n- Purpose: Third-party-derived source treated as owned code.\n- Location: `src/components/motion-primitives/text-effect.tsx` (ADR-0007). Only consumer is the hero role-line effect.\n- Depends on: `motion/react`, `src/lib/utils.ts` (`cn`).\n- Lint: `no-restricted-imports` is turned off for this folder (`.oxlintrc.json`, override `**/motion-primitives/**`).\n\n**Animation layer:**\n- Purpose: The single seam between the app and animation libraries.\n- Location: `src/anim/`\n- Contains:\n  - Barrel `src/anim/index.ts` exports `ThemeThumb`, `MotionPreferenceProvider`, `useMotionPreference`, `springs`, `durations`, `easeOut`, `MotionFeatures`, `ToastPresence`, `NavIndicator`, `PillMorph`.\n  - `declarative/` (motion-owned): `ToastPresence.tsx`, `NavIndicator.tsx`, `PillMorph.tsx`.\n  - `physics/` (react-spring-owned): `ThemeThumb.tsx`.\n  - `featuresStatus.ts` (module-level loaded flag and subscribers), `features.ts` (`domMax` feature bundle), `MotionFeatures.tsx`, `MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`, `useMotionPreference.ts`, `tokens.ts`.\n- Depends on: `motion/react`, `motion/react-m`, `@react-spring/web`, `src/anim/tokens.ts`. Must not import sections, layout, or content (enforced by `.oxlintrc.json` override for `**/anim/**`).\n\n**Hooks and lib:**\n- `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts` (scroll-spy), `src/hooks/useTheme.ts`, `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts` exports `pickActive` for unit tests.\n- `src/lib/theme.ts` (theme read/write/subscribe, storage fallbacks), `src/lib/utils.ts` (`cn`), `src/lib/dates.ts`, `src/lib/copy.ts`.\n\n**Styles:**\n- `src/styles/index.css`: Tailwind import, dark variant, theme mapping.\n- `src/styles/tokens.css`: five role colors per theme, derived tokens via `color-mix(in oklch, ...)`.\n- `src/styles/base.css`: resets, focus ring, reduced-motion guard, fonts.\n- `src/styles/interactions.css`, `src/styles/not-found.css`.\n\n**Content (data):**\n- Location: `src/content/`\n- Contains: `schema.ts`, `profile.ts`, `experience.ts`, `projects.ts`, `skills.ts`, `education.ts`, `contacts.ts`, `images.ts`, `index.ts`.\n- Depends on: `src/content/schema.ts` types and image imports from `src/assets/images/`.\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx` only.\n\n## Data Flow\n\n### Primary Request Path (initial page load)\n\n1. The browser requests `index.html`. The inline script in `<head>` reads `localStorage` key `theme` and falls back to `prefers-color-scheme`, then sets `data-theme` on `<html>` before first paint (`index.html`, lines 13 to 28).\n2. The module script `src/main.tsx` loads the Archivo font CSS and `src/styles/index.css`, then calls `createRoot(...).render(<App />)` inside `<StrictMode>`.\n3. `App` renders `Providers`, which mounts `MotionPreferenceProvider` (`src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`), then `MotionFeatures` (`src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx`), then `ToastProvider` (`src/components/ui/Toast.tsx`).\n4. `Page` (`src/app/Page.tsx`) reads `siteContent` from `src/content/index.ts`, derives `email` from the `kind: 'email'` contact, and filters the other contacts into `otherLinks`.\n5. `Page` calls `useActiveSection(SECTION_IDS)` once, then renders `SiteHeader`, `<main id=\"main\">` with sections, `Footer`, and `PillNav`. Both nav components receive `activeId` and `onNavigate={lockTo}`.\n\n### Motion Feature Loading\n\n1. `MotionFeatures` passes a loader to `LazyMotion`. The loader calls `import('./features')` and resolves `domMax` (`src/anim/features.ts`).\n2. After the chunk loads, `markFeaturesLoaded()` (`src/anim/featuresStatus.ts`) flips a module flag and notifies subscribers, which lets `ToastPresence` and `PillMorph` start using `m.*` components.\n3. When the visitor prefers reduced motion at mount, the loader waits on a promise that only resolves when the preference turns off. The chunk is never downloaded for those visitors.\n4. Otherwise the feature chunk is warmed with `requestIdleCallback` (fallback `setTimeout` 1500 ms).\n\n### Scroll-spy\n\n1. `useActiveSection` (`src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`) creates one `IntersectionObserver` over the section ids. `rootMargin` is built from the sticky nav height and the `-60%` bottom band.\n2. `pickActive` chooses the last section in DOM order that touches the band, or the last section at page bottom.\n3. Clicking a nav item calls `lockTo(id)`, which sets the active id at once and ignores observer updates until `scrollend`, a quiet period, or a 3 s cap.\n\n### Theme Toggle\n\n1. `ThemeToggle` (`src/components/ui/ThemeToggle.tsx`) calls `useTheme().toggle`, which calls `applyTheme` in `src/lib/theme.ts`.\n2. `applyTheme` writes `data-theme` first, then writes `localStorage` (try/catch). If storage is blocked, the choice lasts for the session.\n3. `subscribeTheme` observes `data-theme` mutations, `storage` events from other tabs, and OS scheme changes while nothing is stored. `useSyncExternalStore` re-renders consumers.\n4. `ThemeThumb` (`src/anim/physics/ThemeThumb.tsx`) animates the toggle thumb with react-spring.\n\n### Email Copy\n\n1. `Page` derives the address; `Hero` and `Contact` call `useCopyEmail(address)` (`src/components/ui/useCopyEmail.ts`) and show feedback through `useToast`.\n\n### Project Detail\n\n1. A click or tap in `src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx` sets local state, which opens `ProjectDialog` (`src/sections/Projects/ProjectDialog.tsx`), a native `<dialog>` (ADR-0009).\n\n**State Management:**\n- Theme: `html[data-theme]` is the single source of truth. `useTheme` reads it; `src/lib/theme.ts` owns writes. Module-level `unsavedChoice` flag in `src/lib/theme.ts` tracks blocked storage.\n- Motion preference: `useMotionPreference` (`src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts`) returns `{ reduced }`.\n- Active section: `useActiveSection` local state and refs.\n- Toast: `ToastProvider` context.\n- Everything else: props from `Page`.\n\n## Key Abstractions\n\n**`siteContent`:**\n- Purpose: Single typed content object consumed by `Page`.\n- Examples: `src/content/index.ts`, `src/content/schema.ts`.\n- Pattern: Each module exports a typed constant using `satisfies` against schema types.\n\n**Animation barrel:**\n- Purpose: Only permitted path to animation features from outside `src/anim/`.\n- Examples: `src/anim/index.ts`.\n- Pattern: Semantic components (`ToastPresence`, `NavIndicator`, `PillMorph`, `ThemeThumb`) rather than library primitives. Rule: one library per element or property.\n\n**Motion preference:**\n- Purpose: One source for reduced-motion behavior across motion, react-spring, and CSS.\n- Examples: `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts`, `src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx` (calls `MotionConfig` and `Globals.assign({ skipAnimation })`).\n- Pattern: Context provider plus hook. Wrappers render an instant equivalent when reduced and never hide content in initial CSS state.\n\n**Scroll-spy hook:**\n- Purpose: Active section tracking for both desktop and phone nav.\n- Examples: `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`, unit tests in `tests/unit/active-section.test.ts`.\n- Pattern: Pure selector (`pickActive`) plus a stateful hook around IntersectionObserver.\n\n**Theme store:**\n- Purpose: Light or dark, consistent across components and tabs.\n- Examples: `src/lib/theme.ts`, `src/hooks/useTheme.ts`.\n- Pattern: `useSyncExternalStore` over a DOM attribute.\n\n## Entry Points\n\n**HTML entry (main):**\n- Location: `index.html`\n- Triggers: Browser request to the site root (or base path).\n- Responsibilities: Head metadata, inline theme-init script, static hero shell and section headings, module script `/src/main.tsx`.\n\n**HTML entry (not found):**\n- Location: `404.html`\n- Triggers: GitHub Pages serves it for unknown paths (`docs/architecture.md`, Build and deploy).\n- Responsibilities: Static not-found page. Second Rolldown input in `vite.config.ts`. Styles in `src/styles/not-found.css`.\n\n**JS entry:**\n- Location: `src/main.tsx`\n- Triggers: The module script in `index.html`.\n- Responsibilities: Font import, global CSS import, React root creation.\n\n**Build entry:**\n- Location: `vite.config.ts`\n- Triggers: `npm run dev`, `npm run build`, `npm run preview`.\n\n**Verification entries:**\n- `scripts/check-*.mjs` run from `npm run verify` and CI.\n- `tests/unit/*` (Vitest) and `tests/e2e/*` (Playwright).\n\n## Architectural Constraints\n\n- **Threading:** Single-threaded browser main thread. No Web Workers in `src/`.\n- **Global state:** Module-level mutable state exists in `src/lib/theme.ts` (`unsavedChoice`) and `src/anim/featuresStatus.ts` (loaded flag and subscriber set). The DOM attribute `html[data-theme]` is global shared state written by `src/lib/theme.ts` and the inline script in `index.html`. `localStorage` key `theme` is shared across tabs.\n- **Circular imports:** Not observed in the analyzed files. The lint boundary rules prevent sections and layout from being imported by `src/anim/`.\n- **Import boundaries (enforced in CI through oxlint):**\n  - Only `src/app/Page.tsx` imports `@/content`.\n  - Only `src/anim/**` and `src/components/motion-primitives/**` import `motion/react` or `@react-spring/web`.\n  - Animation is imported only through the `@/anim` barrel.\n- **Reduced motion:** Lazy feature chunk is withheld for reduced-motion visitors, so the initial bundle is the only download for them.\n- **Initial JS budget:** 150 kB gzipped (`scripts/check-js-budget.mjs`).\n- **No raw colors in components:** Enforced by `scripts/check-colors.mjs`. Use semantic Tailwind utilities.\n- **Static only:** No router, no server, no data fetching. Adding any runtime network call changes the deployment model.\n\n## Anti-Patterns\n\nThese are the patterns the lint rules and gates are designed to reject. Current code follows them.\n\n### Importing content data outside the page\n\n**What happens:** A section or component imports `@/content` or `src/content/*` directly to read copy.\n**Why it's wrong:** It breaks the layer rule in `docs/architecture.md` and makes the component non-reusable with props.\n**Do this instead:** Add a prop to the section and pass it from `src/app/Page.tsx`, for example `<Skills groups={siteContent.skills} />` (`src/app/Page.tsx`, line 32).\n\n### Importing motion libraries outside `src/anim`\n\n**What happens:** A section imports `motion/react` or `@react-spring/web` directly for an effect.\n**Why it's wrong:** Reduced-motion handling, lazy loading, and the one-library-per-element rule live in `src/anim/`. Bypassing them causes inconsistent behavior.\n**Do this instead:** Add a semantic wrapper in `src/anim/declarative/` or `src/anim/physics/`, export it from `src/anim/index.ts`, and import it from the barrel (`src/components/ui/Toast.tsx` imports `ToastPresence` from `@/anim`).\n\n### Writing theme state outside `applyTheme`\n\n**What happens:** Code sets `data-theme` or `localStorage.theme` directly.\n**Why it's wrong:** Skips the blocked-storage flag and the `subscribeTheme` sync, so consumers and other tabs drift.\n**Do this instead:** Call `applyTheme` from `src/lib/theme.ts`, or call `useTheme().toggle` (`src/hooks/useTheme.ts`).\n\n### Hard-coding copy or order in components\n\n**What happens:** Text or item order is written in a component instead of the content modules.\n**Why it's wrong:** Violates the copy inventory rule (`docs/architecture.md`, Content model) and the featured-flag ordering rule.\n**Do this instead:** Change the typed module in `src/content/` and let the schema check it.\n\n## Error Handling\n\n**Strategy:** Fail soft on browser capability gaps. Components keep rendering with static fallbacks rather than throwing.\n\n**Patterns:**\n- Storage access is wrapped in `try/catch` in `src/lib/theme.ts` (`applyTheme`, `storedTheme`), and the inline script in `index.html` also uses `try/catch`.\n- `matchMedia` failures fall back to light theme in `src/lib/theme.ts` (`systemTheme`, `subscribeTheme`).\n- A failed motion feature load returns a promise that never settles (`src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx`, `never`), so `m` components stay static and no unhandled rejection occurs.\n- `useActiveSection` returns `activeId: null` when `IntersectionObserver` is unavailable.\n- `hashTarget` in `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts` ignores malformed `decodeURIComponent` input.\n- `src/main.tsx` uses a non-null assertion on `#root`. The HTML shell always provides it.\n\n## Cross-Cutting Concerns\n\n**Logging:** None in the app. No `console` logging is part of the runtime design.\n**Validation:** Compile-time via `satisfies` and the `ContactLink`, `Project`, and other types in `src/content/schema.ts`. Build-time via the `check:*` scripts. Runtime validation of content is not performed.\n**Authentication:** Not applicable. The site has no accounts.\n**Accessibility:** Skip link (`src/layout/SkipLink.tsx`), `<main id=\"main\" tabIndex={-1}>` in `index.html` and `src/app/Page.tsx`, reduced-motion guard in `src/styles/base.css`, focus ring tokens in `src/styles/tokens.css`. Contrast enforced by `scripts/check-contrast.mjs`.\n\n---\n\n*Architecture analysis: 2026-11-20*\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2449,"cache_read_input_tokens":74545,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2449,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":6,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global","speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_01P6injfu4A7jKBDgyJx5ZdV","agent_id":"ad5c7da0700dad6eb","session_id":"4ee6aae5-dd1e-4837-a862-0bbe2709daff","uuid":"799a4cd8-4ac0-411d-aa14-9b0dcd003436","timestamp":"2026-11-20T06:22:42.342Z","request_id":"req_011ChDhTzV1RoKS31XrdnrWZ","subagent_type":"gsd-codebase-mapper","task_description":"Map codebase tech and arch"}

Composition root: App.tsx, Providers.tsx, Page.tsx\n├── sections/              # One folder per page section\n│   ├── Hero/Hero.tsx\n│   ├── Experience/Experience.tsx\n│   ├── Projects/          # Projects.tsx, ProjectDialog.tsx, Sleeve.tsx, project-types.ts\n│   ├── About/About.tsx\n│   ├── Skills/Skills.tsx\n│   ├── Education/Education.tsx\n│   └── Contact/Contact.tsx\n├── layout/                # Public barrel\n│   ├── declarative/       # motion-owned wrappers (ToastPresence, NavIndicator, PillMorph)\n│   └── physics/           # react-spring-owned wrappers (ThemeThumb)\n├── content/               # Typed copy and data (imported only by app/Page.tsx)\n├── hooks/                 # useActiveSection.ts, useTheme.ts\n├── lib/                   # theme.ts, utils.ts (cn), dates.ts, copy.ts\n├── styles/                # index.css, tokens.css, base.css, interactions.css, not-found.css\n├── assets/images/         # Source images imported as modules (processed by vite-imagetools)\n└── types/                 # imagetools.d.ts module typings\n```\n\nTests:\n\n```\ntests/\n├── unit/                  # Vitest specs (*.test.ts / *.test.tsx)\n├── e2e/                   # Playwright specs (*.spec.ts), global-setup.ts\n│   └── mobile/            # Mobile-only specs (pill.spec.ts, ten-second.spec.ts)\n├── fixtures/              # Negative and positive fixtures for lint and gate tests\n│   ├── anim/\n│   ├── app/\n│   ├── motion-primitives/\n│   └── sections/\n└── setup.ts               # Vitest setup\n```\n\nDocs:\n\n```\ndocs/\n├── architecture.md        # Architecture source of truth (layers, import rules, data flow)\n├── deploy-runbook.md      # Deploy steps, cutover, rollback\n├── contrast-matrix.md     # Token contrast record\n└── adr/                   # 0001 … 0010 decision records plus README.md index\n```\n\n## Directory Purposes\n\n**`src/app/`:**\n- Purpose: Composition root only.\n- Contains: `App.tsx` (renders `Providers` then `Page`), `Providers.tsx` (context providers), `Page.tsx` (the only file that imports `@/content`).\n- Key files: `src/app/Page.tsx`, `src/app/Providers.tsx`.\n\n**`src/sections/`:**\n- Purpose: One folder per visible section. Each section receives props and returns JSX.\n- Contains: `<Name>/<Name>.tsx` components, with helper files when needed (`Projects/ProjectDialog.tsx`, `Projects/Sleeve.tsx`, `Projects/project-types.ts`).\n- Key files: `src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx`, `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx`, `src/sections/Contact/Contact.tsx`.\n\n**`src/layout/`:**\n- Purpose: Header, mobile pill nav, footer, layout primitives, and nav definitions.\n- Key files: `src/layout/SiteHeader.tsx`, `src/layout/PillNav.tsx`, `src/layout/nav-items.ts` (defines `SECTION_IDS` and `NAV_ITEMS`).\n\n**`src/components/ui/`:**\n- Purpose: Reusable controls, toast system, clipboard hook, and icons.\n- Key files: `src/components/ui/Toast.tsx`, `src/components/ui/useToast.ts`, `src/components/ui/ThemeToggle.tsx`, `src/components/ui/CtaLink.tsx`, `src/components/ui/useCopyEmail.ts`.\n- Icons: `src/components/ui/icons/*.tsx` (`GitHubIcon`, `LinkedInIcon`, `InstagramIcon`, `CloseIcon`, `ChevronLeftIcon`, `ChevronRightIcon`, `DownloadIcon`).\n\n**`src/components/motion-primitives/`:**\n- Purpose: Vendored primitive source (ADR-0007). Treated as owned code.\n- Key files: `src/components/motion-primitives/text-effect.tsx` (used by the hero role-line effect).\n\n**`src/anim/`:**\n- Purpose: The only animation import path. Consumers use `@/anim`.\n- Key files: `src/anim/index.ts` (barrel), `src/anim/tokens.ts` (springs and durations), `src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx`, `src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`, `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts`, `src/anim/declarative/*.tsx`, `src/anim/physics/ThemeThumb.tsx`.\n\n**`src/content/`:**\n- Purpose: Typed copy and structured data. Each module is one section.\n- Key files: `src/content/schema.ts` (all types), `src/content/index.ts` (assembles `siteContent`), `src/content/profile.ts`, `src/content/experience.ts`, `src/content/projects.ts`, `src/content/skills.ts`, `src/content/education.ts`, `src/content/contacts.ts`, `src/content/images.ts`.\n\n**`src/hooks/`:**\n- Purpose: Cross-component React hooks.\n- Key files: `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts` (scroll-spy and exported `pickActive`), `src/hooks/useTheme.ts`.\n\n**`src/lib/`:**\n- Purpose: Non-React helpers.\n- Key files: `src/lib/theme.ts` (theme storage and subscription), `src/lib/utils.ts` (`cn`), `src/lib/dates.ts`, `src/lib/copy.ts`.\n\n**`src/styles/`:**\n- Purpose: Global CSS and design tokens.\n- Key files: `src/styles/index.css` (Tailwind entry, dark variant, `@theme inline` mapping), `src/styles/tokens.css` (role colors per theme), `src/styles/base.css` (resets, focus ring, reduced-motion guard, fonts), `src/styles/interactions.css`, `src/styles/not-found.css`.\n\n**`src/assets/images/`:**\n- Purpose: Raster images imported as modules.\n- Contents: `about-dj.jpg`, `capitalone.png`, `spectrum.png`, `symbotic.png`.\n- Registry: `src/content/images.ts`.\n\n**`scripts/`:**\n- Purpose: Build-time and CI gates run through `npm run verify`.\n- Key files: `scripts/check-no-phone.mjs`, `scripts/check-images.mjs`, `scripts/check-js-budget.mjs`, `scripts/check-colors.mjs`, `scripts/check-contrast.mjs`, `scripts/check-path-case.mjs`, `scripts/check-qa-shots.mjs`, `scripts/qa-extra-shots.mjs`, and `scripts/contrast-pairs.json`.\n\n**`tests/`:**\n- Purpose: Unit tests (Vitest) and browser tests (Playwright), plus gate fixtures.\n- Key files: `tests/unit/content.test.ts`, `tests/unit/active-section.test.ts`, `tests/unit/theme.test.ts`, `tests/unit/motion-preference.test.tsx`, `tests/unit/boundaries.test.ts`, `tests/e2e/smoke.spec.ts`, `tests/e2e/motion.spec.ts`, `tests/e2e/theme.spec.ts`.\n\n## Key File Locations\n\n**Entry Points:**\n- `index.html`: Main HTML document and first-paint theme script.\n- `404.html`: GitHub Pages not-found document.\n- `src/main.tsx`: JS entry.\n\n**Configuration:**\n- `vite.config.ts`: Build, base path, aliases, two HTML inputs.\n- `tsconfig.app.json`: App typecheck config, `@/*` path alias.\n- `.oxlintrc.json`: Lint rules and import boundaries.\n- `prettier.config.mjs`: Formatting.\n- `vitest.config.ts`, `playwright.config.ts`: Test runners.\n- `package.json`: Scripts and dependency versions.\n\n**Core Logic:**\n- `src/app/Page.tsx`: Page composition and content wiring.\n- `src/anim/index.ts`: Animation API surface.\n- `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`: Scroll-spy.\n- `src/lib/theme.ts`: Theme persistence and sync.\n- `src/content/schema.ts`: Content types.\n\n**Testing:**\n- `tests/unit/`: Vitest specs.\n- `tests/e2e/`: Playwright specs.\n- `tests/fixtures/`: Lint and gate fixtures.\n- `tests/setup.ts`: Vitest setup.\n\n**Documentation:**\n- `docs/architecture.md`: Architecture reference.\n- `docs/adr/`: Decision records. Index in `docs/adr/README.md`.\n- `DESIGN.md`: Design notes at repo root.\n\n## Naming Conventions\n\n**Files:**\n- React components: PascalCase `.tsx`, named after the export. Examples: `src/layout/SiteHeader.tsx`, `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx`, `src/anim/declarative/PillMorph.tsx`.\n- Hooks: `use` prefix, camelCase `.ts`. Examples: `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`, `src/components/ui/useCopyEmail.ts`.\n- Non-component modules: kebab-case or lowercase `.ts`. Examples: `src/layout/nav-items.ts`, `src/sections/Projects/project-types.ts`, `src/anim/tokens.ts`, `src/lib/theme.ts`.\n- Vendored primitive: kebab-case `text-effect.tsx` (`src/components/motion-primitives/`).\n- Icons: `<Name>Icon.tsx` inside `src/components/ui/icons/`.\n- Content modules: lowercase single word, one per section (`src/content/projects.ts`).\n- CSS: lowercase or kebab-case `.css` (`src/styles/base.css`, `src/styles/not-found.css`).\n- Unit tests: kebab-case `tests/unit/<topic>.test.ts` or `.test.tsx` (`tests/unit/active-section.test.ts`).\n- E2E tests: kebab-case `tests/e2e/<topic>.spec.ts` (`tests/e2e/project-dialog.spec.ts`).\n- Gate scripts: `scripts/check-<gate>.mjs` (`scripts/check-path-case.mjs`).\n- ADRs: `docs/adr/NNNN-kebab-title.md` (`docs/adr/0009-native-dialog-for-project-details.md`).\n\n**Directories:**\n- Sections: PascalCase folder per section, with the component inside (`src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx`).\n- Other source folders: lowercase (`src/app`, `src/layout`, `src/hooks`, `src/lib`, `src/anim`, `src/content`).\n- Mixed-case file names in `src/anim/` (e.g. `MotionFeatures.tsx` next to `tokens.ts`) are allowed. `scripts/check-path-case.mjs` enforces that every import matches the on-disk case exactly, which matters on Linux CI.\n\n**Imports:**\n- Use the `@/` alias for `src/` (`@/anim`, `@/layout/nav-items`, `@/components/ui/Toast`). Example: `src/app/Page.tsx`.\n- Import animation only through `@/anim`. Do not import `motion/react` or `@react-spring/web` outside `src/anim/` and `src/components/motion-primitives/`.\n- Import content data only in `src/app/Page.tsx`.\n\n## Where to Add New Code\n\n**New section:**\n- Component: `src/sections/<Name>/<Name>.tsx`. Props typed from `src/content/schema.ts`.\n- Data: a new module in `src/content/` (for example `src/content/<name>.ts`), added to `siteContent` in `src/content/index.ts`.\n- Wiring: add the section to `src/app/Page.tsx`, and to `SECTION_IDS` and `NAV_ITEMS` in `src/layout/nav-items.ts` if it needs a nav link.\n- Tests: `tests/unit/content.test.ts` for data rules, `tests/e2e/` for navigation or visible behavior.\n\n**New layout or chrome component:**\n- Implementation: `src/layout/<Name>.tsx`.\n- Props from `Page` only. Do not import content here.\n\n**New reusable UI control:**\n- Implementation: `src/components/ui/<Name>.tsx`.\n- Icons: `src/components/ui/icons/<Name>Icon.tsx` as inline SVG (ADR-0010). Do not add an icon package.\n\n**New animation effect:**\n- Declarative (mount, exit, layout, in-view): `src/anim/declarative/<Name>.tsx`.\n- Velocity or pointer physics: `src/anim/physics/<Name>.tsx`.\n- Export it from `src/anim/index.ts`.
